Effects of COVID-19 Control Policies on Malaria Surveillance: A Comparative Analysis of North and South Korea.

Effects of COVID-19 Control Policies on Malaria Surveillance: A Comparative Analysis of North and South Korea.
作者： Jong-Hun Kim, Jisun Sung
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
Both South and North Korea are identified by the World Health Organization (WHO) as having the potential to eliminate malaria, but the increase in malaria cases during the COVID-19 response has prompted an investigation into the reasons behind this unexpected rise.
设计与对象
使用世界卫生组织的分析框架,对来自国家和国际 监测 系统的流行病学、昆虫​​学和气象数据进行了分析,以评估朝鲜和韩国疟疾死灰复燃的驱动因素。
核心方法
该研究利用可在消除阶段有效应用的世界卫生组织分析框架,通过分析来自国家和国际 监测 系统的流行病学、昆虫​​学和气象数据,评估了朝鲜和韩国疟疾死灰复燃的驱动因素。
主要结果
In North Korea, border closures caused a severe shortage of rapid diagnostic test kits, reducing malaria testing by over 90% by 2021, while confirmed cases rose from 1819 in 2020 to 3160 in 2023. In South Korea, public health resources were diverted to COVID-19, weakening malaria control activities, with response time for epidemiological investigations increasing from 1.3 days in 2019 to 4.3 days in 2021, and confirmed cases rising from 274 in 2021 to 673 in 2023.
解释与创新
The authors interpreted that COVID-19-related disruptions, including supply chain failures and the diversion of human resources, impaired malaria control in countries on the Korean Peninsula, as there were no meaningful changes in the drivers of malaria resurgence, including importation of malaria parasites or human susceptibility.
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,在大流行期间维持基本卫生服务对于预防消除阶段国家的疟疾死灰复燃至关重要,这凸显了对有弹性的 监测 系统和供应链的需求。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究依赖于 监测 数据,不包括主要数据收集或随机对照试验,其局限性包括潜在的漏报和无法明确建立因果关系。

Effects of COVID-19 Control Policies on Malaria Surveillance: A Comparative Analysis of North and South Korea.

Authors: Jong-Hun Kim, Jisun Sung
Original abstract (English)
PURPOSE: Both South and North Korea are identified by the World Health Organization (WHO) as having the potential to eliminate malaria. However, the increase in malaria cases during the COVID-19 response has prompted an investigation into the reasons behind this unexpected rise. MATERIALS AND METHODS: Epidemiological, entomological, and meteorological data from national and international surveillance systems were analyzed. Using the WHO's analytical framework, which can be effectively applied during the elimination phase, we assessed drivers of malaria resurgence in North and South Korea. RESULTS: In North Korea, border closures caused a severe shortage of rapid diagnostic test kits, reducing malaria testing by over 90% by 2021. Microscopy use increased but remained insufficient, while confirmed cases rose from 1819 in 2020 to 3160 in 2023. In South Korea, public health resources were diverted to COVID-19, weakening malaria control activities. The response time for epidemiological investigations increased from 1.3 days in 2019 to 4.3 days in 2021. Relapse cases, which were zero in 2021, increased to eight in 2023, and confirmed cases rose from 274 in 2021 to 673 in 2023. There were no meaningful changes in the drivers of malaria resurgence, including importation of malaria parasites or human susceptibility. With no notable shifts in vector density, variations in temperature and precipitation did not explain the rise in either country, and there were no reports of emerging resistance to insecticides or antimalarial drugs. CONCLUSION: These COVID-19-related disruptions, including supply chain failures and the diversion of human resources, impaired malaria control in countries on the Korean Peninsula.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Both South and North Korea are identified by the World Health Organization (WHO) as having the potential to eliminate malaria, but the increase in malaria cases during the COVID-19 response has prompted an investigation into the reasons behind this unexpected rise.
Design and population
Epidemiological, entomological, and meteorological data from national and international surveillance systems were analyzed to assess drivers of malaria resurgence in North and South Korea using the WHO's analytical framework.
Core methods
Using the WHO's analytical framework, which can be effectively applied during the elimination phase, the study assessed drivers of malaria resurgence in North and South Korea by analyzing epidemiological, entomological, and meteorological data from national and international surveillance systems.
Main results
In North Korea, border closures caused a severe shortage of rapid diagnostic test kits, reducing malaria testing by over 90% by 2021, while confirmed cases rose from 1819 in 2020 to 3160 in 2023. In South Korea, public health resources were diverted to COVID-19, weakening malaria control activities, with response time for epidemiological investigations increasing from 1.3 days in 2019 to 4.3 days in 2021, and confirmed cases rising from 274 in 2021 to 673 in 2023.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that COVID-19-related disruptions, including supply chain failures and the diversion of human resources, impaired malaria control in countries on the Korean Peninsula, as there were no meaningful changes in the drivers of malaria resurgence, including importation of malaria parasites or human susceptibility.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform that maintaining essential health services during pandemics is crucial to prevent malaria resurgence in elimination-phase countries, highlighting the need for resilient surveillance systems and supply chains.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate as the study relied on surveillance data and did not include primary data collection or randomized controlled trials, with limitations including potential underreporting and the inability to establish causality definitively.

Low reporting quality limits the contribution of human organ models to COVID-19 research: a systematic review.

Low reporting quality limits the contribution of human organ models to COVID-19 research: a systematic review.
作者： Maren Hülsemann, S. Kniffert, Anna Löwa, Morris Baumgardt, K. Hönzke, Vallone VF, Drude NI, Thomas Stüdemann, Ahmed S. M. Ali, Maria Arroyo Araujo
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
The COVID-19 pandemic created a unique situation in which researchers repurposed human models of varying complexity to investigate SARS-CoV-2, providing the opportunity to analyse their contribution across organs.
设计与对象
我们对 558 项研究(分为大流行期和大流行后时期)进行了系统回顾,以评估如何应用人体模型来研究 宿主 因素、严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2、免疫应答 的病毒复制,并评估报告质量。
核心方法
该研究评估了如何应用人体模型来研究宿主因素、严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2、免疫应答的病毒复制,并通过对558项研究的系统评价来评估报告质量。
主要结果
我们的分析揭示了大流行后研究中仅部分缓解的实质性局限性,包括结果测量的异质性、模型特征和实验程序的不完整记录以及可变的报告质量,这些都与交叉研究可比性降低、偏倚风险评估更加困难以及证据合成和荟萃分析结果的可靠性和可解释性有限有关。
解释与创新
加强社区驱动的报告标准和方法透明度对于实现强有力的证据合成并充分发挥人体器官模型在未来流行病和转化研究中的潜力至关重要。
科研与公卫意义
The findings inform the need for improved reporting standards to enhance the reliability and interpretability of evidence from human organ models in COVID-19 research, which is crucial for future pandemic preparedness and translational research.
局限与证据强度
The study identified substantial limitations in the reporting quality of human organ models used in COVID-19 research, including heterogeneity of outcome measures, incomplete documentation, and variable reporting quality, which reduced cross-study comparability and limited the reliability of evidence synthesis; the evidence strength is moderate due to the systematic review approach but is constrained by these reporting limitations.

Low reporting quality limits the contribution of human organ models to COVID-19 research: a systematic review.

Authors: Maren Hülsemann, S. Kniffert, Anna Löwa, Morris Baumgardt, K. Hönzke, Vallone VF, Drude NI, Thomas Stüdemann, Ahmed S. M. Ali, Maria Arroyo Araujo
Original abstract (English)
Background The COVID-19 pandemic created a unique situation in which researchers repurposed human models of varying complexity to investigate SARS-CoV-2, providing the opportunity to analyse their contribution across organs. Methods We conducted a systematic review of 558 studies, divided into pandemic and post-pandemic periods, to assess how human models were applied to investigate host factors, viral replication of SARS-CoV-2, immune responses, and to evaluate reporting quality. Findings Our analysis revealed substantial limitations that were only partially alleviated in post-pandemic studies. These limitations included heterogeneity of outcome measures, incomplete documentation of model characteristics and experimental procedures, and variable reporting quality, which were associated with reduced cross-study comparability, more difficult risk-of-bias assessment, and limited reliability and interpretability of evidence synthesis and meta-analytical results. Interpretation Strengthening community-driven reporting standards and methodological transparency is essential to enable robust evidence synthesis and to fully realise the potential of human organ models in future pandemics and translational research. Funding The study was supported by Einstein Foundation Berlin; Federal Ministry of Research, Technology and Space; European Union; Else Kröner-Fresenius-Stiftung; Volkswagen Foundation; Charité 3R; Foundation Charité.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The COVID-19 pandemic created a unique situation in which researchers repurposed human models of varying complexity to investigate SARS-CoV-2, providing the opportunity to analyse their contribution across organs.
Design and population
We conducted a systematic review of 558 studies, divided into pandemic and post-pandemic periods, to assess how human models were applied to investigate host factors, viral replication of SARS-CoV-2, immune responses, and to evaluate reporting quality.
Core methods
The study assessed how human models were applied to investigate host factors, viral replication of SARS-CoV-2, immune responses, and evaluated reporting quality through a systematic review of 558 studies.
Main results
Our analysis revealed substantial limitations that were only partially alleviated in post-pandemic studies, including heterogeneity of outcome measures, incomplete documentation of model characteristics and experimental procedures, and variable reporting quality, which were associated with reduced cross-study comparability, more difficult risk-of-bias assessment, and limited reliability and interpretability of evidence synthesis and meta-analytical results.
Interpretation and novelty
Strengthening community-driven reporting standards and methodological transparency is essential to enable robust evidence synthesis and to fully realise the potential of human organ models in future pandemics and translational research.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for improved reporting standards to enhance the reliability and interpretability of evidence from human organ models in COVID-19 research, which is crucial for future pandemic preparedness and translational research.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study identified substantial limitations in the reporting quality of human organ models used in COVID-19 research, including heterogeneity of outcome measures, incomplete documentation, and variable reporting quality, which reduced cross-study comparability and limited the reliability of evidence synthesis; the evidence strength is moderate due to the systematic review approach but is constrained by these reporting limitations.

Long-term Cardiovascular Outcomes in US Medicare Beneficiaries After COVID-19 Hospitalization During the Omicron Era.

Long-term Cardiovascular Outcomes in US Medicare Beneficiaries After COVID-19 Hospitalization During the Omicron Era.
作者： Sérgio R. R. Decker, Yang Song, Issa J Dahabreh, Robert W. Yeh, Dhruv S. Kazi
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
The study aimed to address the knowledge gap regarding long-term cardiovascular outcomes in older adults hospitalized with COVID-19 during the Omicron era, as prior data from the pre-Omicron era indicated an increased risk of major adverse cardiovascular events.
设计与对象
A retrospective cohort study was conducted on Medicare beneficiaries aged ≥65 years hospitalized for COVID-19 during the Omicron era (November 2021-September 2022), the pre-Omicron era (March 2020-November 2021), and a historical influenza cohort (October 2017-September 2018).
核心方法
该研究使用逆概率加权来标准化 队列研究,并比较了 Omicron 和 Omicron 之前的 队列研究 以及流感 队列研究 之间的死亡和主要不良心血管事件的风险。
主要结果
主要结局是全因死亡和心血管事件的综合结果,在 Omicron 队列研究 中发生率为 48.8%,在 Omicron 队列研究 之前为 49.3%,在流感 队列研究 中发生率为 33.4%。与之前的 Omicron 队列研究 相比,Omicron 队列研究 的死亡风险显着降低(34.2% 对比 39.7%),但心肌梗塞、中风、肺栓塞、深静脉血栓和心力衰竭的风险更高。
解释与创新
作者解释说,Omicron 时代的死亡风险低于 Omicron 之前的时代,但主要不良心血管事件的风险更高,这凸显了与 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变异相关的住院后结果的变化。
科研与公卫意义
The findings suggest that cardiovascular prevention strategies should be prioritized for older adults recovering from Omicron-related COVID-19 hospitalizations, as the risk of major adverse cardiovascular events remains elevated compared to influenza.
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和潜在的混杂因素而受到限制,但由于样本量大和标准化的 队列研究 比较,证据充足。

Long-term Cardiovascular Outcomes in US Medicare Beneficiaries After COVID-19 Hospitalization During the Omicron Era.

Authors: Sérgio R. R. Decker, Yang Song, Issa J Dahabreh, Robert W. Yeh, Dhruv S. Kazi
Original abstract (English)
Background COVID-19 hospitalization in the pre-Omicron era was associated with an increased risk of major adverse cardiovascular events, but data from the Omicron era are sparse. We examined the risk of death and major adverse cardiovascular events among older adults hospitalized with COVID-19 during the Omicron era compared with the pre-Omicron era and a historical influenza cohort. Methods Retrospective cohort study of Medicare beneficiaries aged ≥65 years hospitalized for COVID-19 in the Omicron era (November 2021-September2022), COVID-19 in the pre-Omicron era (March 2020-November 2021), and influenza (October 2017-September 2018). The primary outcome was a composite of all-cause death and hospitalization for myocardial infarction, ischemic stroke, transient ischemic attack, pulmonary embolism, deep vein thrombosis, heart failure, and cardiac arrest within 1 year of the index admission. We standardized the cohorts using inverse probability weighting. Results We included 1 184 182 patients (mean age 79 years; 51% women):361 382 in the Omicron cohort, 731 455 in the pre-Omicron cohort, and 91 345 in the influenza cohort. The risk of the primary outcome in the Omicron cohort was lower than in the pre-Omicron cohort (48.8% versus 49.3%; standardized risk difference, -0.6% [95% CI, -0.8% to -0.3%]) but higher than in the influenza cohort (48.8% versus 33.4%; risk difference, 15.4% [15.1%-15.6%]). Compared with the pre-Omicron cohort, the Omicron cohort had a significantly lower risk of death (34.2% versus 39.7%; risk difference, -5.5% [-5.7% to -5.3%]) but a higher risk of myocardial infarction, stroke or transient ischemic attack, pulmonary embolism, deep vein thrombosis, and heart failure. Conclusions Among older adults hospitalized with COVID-19, the risk of death within 1 year was lower, and the risk of major adverse cardiovascular event was higher in the Omicron era than the pre-Omicron era; however, the risk of either outcome was higher than after influenza hospitalization. These findings underscore the need for effective cardiovascular prevention strategies after a COVID-19 hospitalization.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the knowledge gap regarding long-term cardiovascular outcomes in older adults hospitalized with COVID-19 during the Omicron era, as prior data from the pre-Omicron era indicated an increased risk of major adverse cardiovascular events.
Design and population
A retrospective cohort study was conducted on Medicare beneficiaries aged ≥65 years hospitalized for COVID-19 during the Omicron era (November 2021-September 2022), the pre-Omicron era (March 2020-November 2021), and a historical influenza cohort (October 2017-September 2018).
Core methods
The study used inverse probability weighting to standardize the cohorts and compared the risk of death and major adverse cardiovascular events between the Omicron and pre-Omicron cohorts, as well as the influenza cohort.
Main results
The primary outcome, a composite of all-cause death and cardiovascular events, occurred in 48.8% of the Omicron cohort, 49.3% of the pre-Omicron cohort, and 33.4% of the influenza cohort. The Omicron cohort had a significantly lower risk of death (34.2% vs. 39.7%) but a higher risk of myocardial infarction, stroke, pulmonary embolism, deep vein thrombosis, and heart failure compared to the pre-Omicron cohort.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the risk of death was lower in the Omicron era than in the pre-Omicron era, but the risk of major adverse cardiovascular events was higher, highlighting a shift in the post-hospitalization outcomes associated with SARS-CoV-2 variants.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that cardiovascular prevention strategies should be prioritized for older adults recovering from Omicron-related COVID-19 hospitalizations, as the risk of major adverse cardiovascular events remains elevated compared to influenza.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential for confounding factors, but the evidence is strong due to the large sample size and standardized cohort comparison.

Persistence of SARS-CoV-2 RNA Residues in Placenta Tissue Following Maternal COVID-19 Infection During Pregnancy.

Persistence of SARS-CoV-2 RNA Residues in Placenta Tissue Following Maternal COVID-19 Infection During Pregnancy.
作者： Yang Gu, Jie Xu, Xin Gu, David F. Lewis, Krista Queen, Rona S. Scott, Dani Zoorob, John A. Vanchiere, Yuping Wang
摘要中文翻译问题:据报道,在感染 新型冠状病毒感染 的患者的肺外组织中,严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 病毒持续存在时间较长。严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 可感染胎盘组织。然而,人们对 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 病毒在胎盘中的持续存在知之甚少。研究方法:这是一项经 IRB 批准的回顾性研究。对 40 名不同孕龄感染 新型冠状病毒感染 的女性的胎盘组织样本进行了分析。通过原位杂交在胎盘和胎膜上检查严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 RNA 表达。通过免疫染色测定S-蛋白和N-蛋白表达。通过原位杂交检测到严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 RNA表达阳性的29例中的12例也进行了严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 RNA测序。结果:40例中的29例在胎盘中检测到严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 RNA表达,主要定位于合体滋养层,但胎膜中未检测到严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 RNA表达。29例阳性病例中,孕早期感染者4例,孕中期感染者7例,孕晚期或足月感染者18例。从感染到分娩的最长间隔为221天。在 L/D 入院时 新型冠状病毒感染 检测呈阳性的 21 名女性中,18 名 (85.7%) 在胎盘组织中检测到 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 RNA 表达。值得注意的是,19 例(L/D 入院时检测呈阴性)中有 11 例(57.9%)在胎盘绒毛组织中检测到病毒 RNA 表达。在两个 COVID(+)新生儿胎盘的绒毛核心血管内皮中也检测到病毒 RNA 表达,在合体滋养层中相应的 S 蛋白和 N 蛋白阳性表达。29例严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 RNA表达阳性的病例中,27例未检测到S蛋白和N蛋白表达。在所有 12 个检查病例中均鉴定出了部分 RNA 依赖性 RNA 聚合酶 (RdRp) 区域序列。结论:这些发现提供了令人信服的证据,表明 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 遗传物质在母体感染后很长时间内可以在胎盘组织中持续存在,这可能有助于怀孕期间母胎界面的持续炎症环境。检测到部分 RdRp 序列的现象将为测试样本中检测不到的 S 和 N 蛋白表达提供可能的解释。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
Prolonged SARS-CoV-2 viral persistence was reported in extrapulmonary tissues in patients infected with COVID-19, but little is known about SARS-CoV-2 viral persistence in the placenta.
设计与对象
This is a retrospective and IRB approved study analyzing placental tissue samples from 40 women infected with COVID-19 at various gestational age.
核心方法
胎盘和胎膜原位杂交检测严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 RNA表达,免疫染色检测S蛋白和N蛋白表达,29例RNA检测阳性的12例进行严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 RNA测序。
主要结果
40例中29例胎盘中检测到严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 RNA表达,主要定位于合体滋养层,从感染到分娩最长间隔为221天;在两个 COVID (+) 新生儿胎盘的绒毛核心血管内皮中也检测到病毒 RNA 表达。
解释与创新
这些发现提供了令人信服的证据,表明 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 遗传物质在母体感染后很长时间内可以在胎盘组织中持续存在,可能导致怀孕期间母胎界面的持续炎症环境。
科研与公卫意义
The persistence of SARS-CoV-2 RNA in placental tissue may have implications for understanding the long-term effects of COVID-19 on pregnancy outcomes and fetal development, warranting further investigation into potential clinical monitoring strategies.
局限与证据强度
尽管部分 RdRp 序列的检测为无法检测到的蛋白质表达提供了可能的解释,但该研究因其回顾性设计以及大多数情况下缺乏 S 蛋白和 N 蛋白表达而受到限制;由于样本量和方法学限制,证据强度中等。

Persistence of SARS-CoV-2 RNA Residues in Placenta Tissue Following Maternal COVID-19 Infection During Pregnancy.

Authors: Yang Gu, Jie Xu, Xin Gu, David F. Lewis, Krista Queen, Rona S. Scott, Dani Zoorob, John A. Vanchiere, Yuping Wang
Original abstract (English)
PROBLEM: Prolonged SARS-CoV-2 viral persistence was reported in extrapulmonary tissues in patients infected with COVID-19. SARS-CoV-2 can infect placental tissue. However, little is known about SARS-CoV-2 viral persistence in the placenta. METHOD OF STUDY: This is a retrospective and IRB approved study. Placental tissue samples from 40 women infected with COVID-19 at various gestational age were analyzed. SARS-CoV-2 RNA expression was examined by in situ hybridization on placental disk and fetal membrane. S-protein and N-protein expression was determined by immunostaining. SARS-CoV-2 RNA sequencing was also performed in 12 out of the 29 cases in which SARS-CoV-2 RNA expression was detected positive by in situ hybridization. RESULTS: SARS-CoV-2 RNA expression was detected in placental disk, predominantly localized in syncytiotrophoblasts in 29 out of the 40 cases, but not in fetal membrane. Among the 29 positive cases, 4 infected in the first trimester, 7 in the second trimester, and 18 in the third trimester or at term. The longest interval from infection to delivery was 221 days. Among the 21 women who tested positive for COVID-19 at L/D admission, 18 (85.7%) detected SARS-CoV-2 RNA expression in placental tissue. Notably, 11 (57.9%) of 19 cases (negative test at L/D admission) detected viral RNA expression in placental villous tissue. Viral RNA expression was also detected in villous core vessel endothelium in two COVID (+) newborn placentas, with corresponding positive S-protein and N-protein expression in syncytiotrophoblasts. S-protein and N-protein expression was undetectable in 27 out of the 29 cases with positive detection of SARS-CoV-2 RNA expression. Partial RNA-dependent RNA polymerase (RdRp) region sequence was identified in all the 12 cases examined. CONCLUSIONS: These findings provide compelling evidence that SARS-CoV-2 genetic material can persist in placental tissue long after maternal infection, which may potentially contribute to a sustained inflammatory environment at the maternal-fetal interface during pregnancy. The phenomenon of partial RdRp sequence detected would provide a possible explanation for undetectable S- and N-protein expression in testing specimens.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Prolonged SARS-CoV-2 viral persistence was reported in extrapulmonary tissues in patients infected with COVID-19, but little is known about SARS-CoV-2 viral persistence in the placenta.
Design and population
This is a retrospective and IRB approved study analyzing placental tissue samples from 40 women infected with COVID-19 at various gestational age.
Core methods
SARS-CoV-2 RNA expression was examined by in situ hybridization on placental disk and fetal membrane, S-protein and N-protein expression was determined by immunostaining, and SARS-CoV-2 RNA sequencing was performed in 12 out of the 29 cases with positive RNA detection.
Main results
SARS-CoV-2 RNA expression was detected in placental disk, predominantly localized in syncytiotrophoblasts in 29 out of the 40 cases, with the longest interval from infection to delivery being 221 days; viral RNA expression was also detected in villous core vessel endothelium in two COVID (+) newborn placentas.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings provide compelling evidence that SARS-CoV-2 genetic material can persist in placental tissue long after maternal infection, potentially contributing to a sustained inflammatory environment at the maternal-fetal interface during pregnancy.
Scientific and public-health significance
The persistence of SARS-CoV-2 RNA in placental tissue may have implications for understanding the long-term effects of COVID-19 on pregnancy outcomes and fetal development, warranting further investigation into potential clinical monitoring strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and the absence of S-protein and N-protein expression in most cases, though the detection of partial RdRp sequence provides a possible explanation for undetectable protein expression; the evidence strength is moderate due to the sample size and methodological constraints.

Post-COVID-19 Conditions and Vaccination in Children and Adolescents during the Omicron Outbreak: A Case-Control Study in Sapporo, Japan.

Post-COVID-19 Conditions and Vaccination in Children and Adolescents during the Omicron Outbreak: A Case-Control Study in Sapporo, Japan.
作者： Satoshi Sunohara, Takashi Kimura, Hana Wakasa, Isaku Kurotori, Satoshi Oshima, Shun Ozawa, Masayuki Saijo, Hiroyasu Iso, Akiko Tamakoshi
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
The study aimed to investigate the association between vaccination and post-COVID-19 conditions in children and adolescents during the Omicron outbreak, given that persistent symptoms were more frequent among COVID-19-positive individuals, particularly smell and taste dysfunction.
设计与对象
A case-control study was conducted in Sapporo, Japan, to examine the relationship between pre-infection vaccination and post-COVID-19 conditions in children and adolescents during the Omicron outbreak.
核心方法
The study compared the frequency of post-COVID-19 conditions between vaccinated and unvaccinated individuals who tested positive for COVID-19, focusing on specific symptoms such as smell/taste dysfunction, nausea, and hair loss.
主要结果
Vaccination before infection was associated with a lower risk of post-COVID-19 conditions, particularly smell/taste dysfunction, nausea, and hair loss.
解释与创新
The authors interpreted that vaccination may reduce the risk of developing post-COVID-19 conditions in children and adolescents, with a novel focus on the Omicron variant and specific symptom associations.
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,疫苗接种 可以减轻儿科人群出现长期新冠肺炎后症状的风险,为 疫苗接种 策略和 Omicron 暴发 期间的新冠肺炎后护理提供信息。
局限与证据强度
由于病例对照设计和对自我报告症状的依赖,证据强度适中,这可能会引入回忆偏差,尽管该研究提供了有关 疫苗接种 益处的宝贵见解。

Post-COVID-19 Conditions and Vaccination in Children and Adolescents during the Omicron Outbreak: A Case-Control Study in Sapporo, Japan.

Authors: Satoshi Sunohara, Takashi Kimura, Hana Wakasa, Isaku Kurotori, Satoshi Oshima, Shun Ozawa, Masayuki Saijo, Hiroyasu Iso, Akiko Tamakoshi
Original abstract (English)
Persistent symptoms attributed to post-COVID-19 conditions were more frequent among COVID-19-positive individuals, especially smell and taste dysfunction. Vaccination before infection was associated with a lower risk of post-COVID-19 conditions, particularly smell/taste dysfunction, nausea, and hair loss.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the association between vaccination and post-COVID-19 conditions in children and adolescents during the Omicron outbreak, given that persistent symptoms were more frequent among COVID-19-positive individuals, particularly smell and taste dysfunction.
Design and population
A case-control study was conducted in Sapporo, Japan, to examine the relationship between pre-infection vaccination and post-COVID-19 conditions in children and adolescents during the Omicron outbreak.
Core methods
The study compared the frequency of post-COVID-19 conditions between vaccinated and unvaccinated individuals who tested positive for COVID-19, focusing on specific symptoms such as smell/taste dysfunction, nausea, and hair loss.
Main results
Vaccination before infection was associated with a lower risk of post-COVID-19 conditions, particularly smell/taste dysfunction, nausea, and hair loss.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that vaccination may reduce the risk of developing post-COVID-19 conditions in children and adolescents, with a novel focus on the Omicron variant and specific symptom associations.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that vaccination could mitigate the risk of long-term post-COVID symptoms in pediatric populations, informing vaccination strategies and post-COVID care during Omicron outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the case-control design and reliance on self-reported symptoms, which may introduce recall bias, though the study provides valuable insights into vaccination benefits.

记忆 B 细胞对 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变体的可调节免疫印记 2255300

Tunable Immune Imprinting of Memory B Cells to SARS-CoV-2 Variants 2255300
作者： Yuezhou Chen, Youyi Wang, Nuo Gong, Changxuan Chen, Chun Li, Zhanguo Li
摘要中文翻译摘要 简介 免疫印记,历史上被称为“原始抗原原罪”,使记忆 B 细胞 (MBC) 记忆偏向祖先菌株的交叉反应表位,从而限制了 疫苗 对快速进化变体的适应性。免疫印记是抗原暴露的固定结果还是可以通过 宿主 免疫因子调节仍不清楚。系统性红斑狼疮 (SLE) 患者的慢性炎症和免疫抑制状况会影响 MBC 回忆反应期间抗体分泌细胞的形成,但这些 宿主 内在状况是否可以调节 MBC 表位靶向和免疫印记以响应抗原性遥远变异仍不清楚。方法 我们招募了时间紧密匹配的健康个体和 SLE 患者的 队列研究,他们接受了三剂祖先灭活的 疫苗,随后经历了同步的 Omicron BA.5/BF.7 突破性感染。我们通过单克隆抗体 (mAb) 表位作图、深度突变扫描 (DMS)、光谱流式细胞术和抗原相关单细胞多组学来表征他们的 MBC 反应。结果健康个体表现出强烈的免疫印记,其特征是对高亲和力交叉反应性 MBC 的强大回忆。相比之下,SLE 患者的印记明显减少,DMS 显示对变异特异性中和表位的识别增强。抗原相关单细胞分析显示 SLE 中 MBC 回忆减弱,与 MBC 再激活减少、BCR 信号传导减弱和炎症特征增强相关。同时,幼稚和 IgM MBC 被招募并成熟为变体特异性 IgG MBC,同时伴随着早期自身反应性和多反应性的下降。结论 我们的研究结果表明,MBC 中的免疫印记是可塑的,炎症和 B 细胞激活信号传导的调节可以重新平衡回忆与从头反应,从而促进变异适应性免疫。资金来源 国家自然科学基金面上项目 课题类别 淋巴细胞分化与外周维持(LYM)
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
免疫印记使记忆 B 细胞记忆偏向祖先菌株,限制了 疫苗 对进化变异的适应性,并且这种印记是否可以通过 宿主 免疫因子调节仍不清楚。
设计与对象
我们招募了时间紧密匹配的健康个体和 SLE 患者的 队列研究,他们接受了三剂祖先灭活的 疫苗,随后经历了同步的 Omicron BA.5/BF.7 突破性感染。
核心方法
我们通过单克隆抗体 (mAb) 表位作图、深度突变扫描 (DMS)、光谱流式细胞术和抗原相关单细胞多组学来表征他们的 MBC 反应。
主要结果
健康个体表现出强烈的免疫印记,对高亲和力交叉反应性 MBC 具有强大的回忆,而 SLE 患者表现出明显减少的印记,对变体特异性中和表位的识别增强,并且 MBC 回忆减弱。
解释与创新
我们的研究结果表明,MBC 中的免疫印记是可塑的,炎症和 B 细胞激活信号传导的调节可以重新平衡回忆与从头反应,从而促进变异适应性免疫。
科研与公卫意义
These insights suggest that targeting inflammatory pathways could enhance vaccine responses against evolving variants, potentially informing strategies to improve COVID-19 vaccine adaptability.
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据强度中等,因为它基于仅抽象的数据,没有详细的方法或统计分析,限制了对普遍性和潜在偏差的评估。

Tunable Immune Imprinting of Memory B Cells to SARS-CoV-2 Variants 2255300

Authors: Yuezhou Chen, Youyi Wang, Nuo Gong, Changxuan Chen, Chun Li, Zhanguo Li
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction Immune imprinting, historically termed “original antigenic sin”, biases memory B cell (MBC) recall toward cross-reactive epitopes of ancestral strains, limiting vaccine adaptability to rapidly evolving variants. Whether immune imprinting is a fixed outcome of antigen exposure or can be modulated by host immune factors remains unclear. Chronic inflammatory and immunosuppressive conditions in systemic lupus erythematosus (SLE) patients affect antibody-secreting cell formation during MBC recall responses, but whether these host-intrinsic conditions can modulate MBC epitope targeting and immune imprinting in response to an antigenically distant variant remains unknown. Methods We recruited tightly time-matched cohorts of healthy individuals and SLE patients who had received three doses of ancestral inactivated vaccine and subsequently experienced synchronized Omicron BA.5/BF.7 breakthrough infection. We characterized their MBC responses through monoclonal antibody (mAb) epitope mapping, deep mutational scanning (DMS), spectral flow cytometry, and antigen-linked single-cell multi-omics. Results Healthy individuals displayed strong immune imprinting, characterized by robust recall of high-affinity cross-reactive MBCs. In contrast, SLE patients exhibited markedly reduced imprinting, with DMS revealing enhanced recognition of variant-specific neutralizing epitopes. Antigen-linked single-cell profiling showed attenuated MBC recall in SLE, associated with reduced MBC reactivation, diminished BCR signaling, and heightened inflammatory signatures. Concurrently, naïve and IgM MBCs were recruited and matured into variant-specific IgG MBCs, accompanied by a decline in early self- and polyreactivity. Conclusion Our findings demonstrate that immune imprinting in MBCs is plastic, and modulation of inflammatory and B cell activation signaling can rebalance recall versus de novo responses to promote variant-adaptable immunity. Funding Source National Natural Science Foundation of China Topic Categories Lymphocyte Differentiation and Peripheral Maintenance (LYM)
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Immune imprinting biases memory B cell recall toward ancestral strains, limiting vaccine adaptability to evolving variants, and whether this imprinting can be modulated by host immune factors remains unclear.
Design and population
We recruited tightly time-matched cohorts of healthy individuals and SLE patients who had received three doses of ancestral inactivated vaccine and subsequently experienced synchronized Omicron BA.5/BF.7 breakthrough infection.
Core methods
We characterized their MBC responses through monoclonal antibody (mAb) epitope mapping, deep mutational scanning (DMS), spectral flow cytometry, and antigen-linked single-cell multi-omics.
Main results
Healthy individuals displayed strong immune imprinting with robust recall of high-affinity cross-reactive MBCs, while SLE patients exhibited markedly reduced imprinting with enhanced recognition of variant-specific neutralizing epitopes and attenuated MBC recall.
Interpretation and novelty
Our findings demonstrate that immune imprinting in MBCs is plastic, and modulation of inflammatory and B cell activation signaling can rebalance recall versus de novo responses to promote variant-adaptable immunity.
Scientific and public-health significance
These insights suggest that targeting inflammatory pathways could enhance vaccine responses against evolving variants, potentially informing strategies to improve COVID-19 vaccine adaptability.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence strength is moderate, as it is based on abstract-only data without detailed methods or statistical analyses, limiting assessment of generalizability and potential biases.

基于 mRNA 的季节性流感和 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 多组分 疫苗,mRNA-1083 在日本 50 岁以上成年人中的免疫原性、反应原性和安全性。

Immunogenicity, reactogenicity, and safety of an mRNA-based seasonal influenza and SARS-CoV-2 multicomponent vaccine, mRNA-1083, in adults aged ≥50 years in Japan.
作者： Lusiné Kostanyan, Hiroyuki Fukase, Alexander Rumyantsev, Koichi Hashizume, Seun Osibajo, Osamu Kogawara
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究通过评估单一基于 mRNA 的多组分 疫苗 是否可以同时为年龄≥50 岁的成年人提供针对季节性流感和 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 的保护,解决了对简化季节性 疫苗接种 的公共卫生需求。
设计与对象
一项亚太地区随机、观察者盲法 3 期试验(第 1 部分在日本)招募了年龄≥50 岁的成年人,他们以 1:1 的比例随机接受 mRNA-1083 加安慰剂或许可的流感 HA 疫苗 加 mRNA-1273,其中 2022 名参与者随机分配,2013 名参与者接受研究干预。
核心方法
参与者接受 mRNA-1083 或活性比较剂;在第29天和第181天评估免疫原性,并在整个过程中监测安全性/反应原性,包括引起的不良反应和严重不良事件。
主要结果
在第 29 天,对于所有评估的流感毒株和 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2,mRNA-1083 引发的 免疫应答 与活性比较剂相比不劣势,并且在总体人群中证明了比较剂匹配的流感毒株和 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 的优越性。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证明 mRNA-1083 可以在单次注射中同时提供针对流感和 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 的保护,代表了一种针对季节性 疫苗接种 的新方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果支持mRNA-1083作为针对年龄≥50岁成人的流感和严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2的潜在单剂量季节性疫苗接种策略,免疫应答在疫苗接种后维持6个月。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于在日本进行的一项具有短期随访的 3 期试验;对其他人群的普遍性和长期安全性需要进一步研究,需要进行适度的置信度评估。

Immunogenicity, reactogenicity, and safety of an mRNA-based seasonal influenza and SARS-CoV-2 multicomponent vaccine, mRNA-1083, in adults aged ≥50 years in Japan.

Authors: Lusiné Kostanyan, Hiroyuki Fukase, Alexander Rumyantsev, Koichi Hashizume, Seun Osibajo, Osamu Kogawara
Original abstract (English)
A multicomponent vaccine targeting seasonal influenza and coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19) may reduce disease burden by providing simultaneous protection in a single injection. We report findings from Part 1 (Japan) of a phase 3, Asia-Pacific, randomized, observer-blind study evaluating immunogenicity, reactogenicity, and safety of mRNA-based multicomponent vaccine mRNA-1083, combining influenza and severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2) antigens. Participants aged ≥50 years were randomized (1:1) to receive mRNA-1083 + placebo or Japan-licensed influenza hemagglutinin (HA) vaccine + mRNA-1273. Overall, 2022 participants were randomized, and 2013 received study intervention. At Day 29, mRNA-1083 elicited noninferior immune responses versus active comparators for all evaluated influenza strains and SARS-CoV-2. Among high-risk participants (aged ≥65 years and 60 to <65 years with ≥1 comorbidity), noninferiority of mRNA-1083 was demonstrated for comparator-matched influenza strains and SARS-CoV-2. In the overall study population, mRNA-1083 demonstrated superiority for the comparator-matched influenza strains and for SARS-CoV-2. At Day 181, immune responses remained above baseline and were comparable to or numerically higher than those elicited by active comparators. Most solicited adverse reactions were grade 1 or 2. There were no cases of myocarditis or pericarditis, and no reported serious adverse events or deaths related to study intervention. Overall, mRNA-1083 demonstrated an acceptable safety profile, eliciting noninferior and superior immune responses against influenza and SARS-CoV-2 in the high-risk and overall study population, respectively. Immune responses were maintained through 6 months post-vaccination. These findings support mRNA-1083 as a single-dose approach to seasonal vaccination against influenza and SARS-CoV-2 in adults ≥50 years. ClinicalTrials.govidentifier: NCT06694389 (https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT06694389).
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the public-health need for simplified seasonal vaccination by evaluating whether a single mRNA-based multicomponent vaccine could provide simultaneous protection against seasonal influenza and SARS-CoV-2 in adults aged ≥50 years.
Design and population
A phase 3, Asia-Pacific, randomized, observer-blind trial (Part 1 in Japan) enrolled adults aged ≥50 years who were randomized 1:1 to receive mRNA-1083 plus placebo or licensed influenza HA vaccine plus mRNA-1273, with 2022 participants randomized and 2013 receiving study intervention.
Core methods
Participants received either mRNA-1083 or active comparators; immunogenicity was assessed at Day 29 and Day 181, and safety/reactogenicity were monitored throughout, including solicited adverse reactions and serious adverse events.
Main results
At Day 29, mRNA-1083 elicited noninferior immune responses versus active comparators for all evaluated influenza strains and SARS-CoV-2, with superiority demonstrated for comparator-matched influenza strains and SARS-CoV-2 in the overall population.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as demonstrating that mRNA-1083 can provide simultaneous protection against influenza and SARS-CoV-2 in a single injection, representing a novel approach to seasonal vaccination.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results support mRNA-1083 as a potential single-dose seasonal vaccination strategy against influenza and SARS-CoV-2 in adults aged ≥50 years, with immune responses maintained through 6 months post-vaccination.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to a single phase 3 trial in Japan with short-term follow-up; generalizability to other populations and long-term safety requires further study, warranting a moderate confidence assessment.

Estimating Time-Inhomogeneous Transition Probabilities from District Level Daily COVID-19 Transmission Data in Sierra Leone

Estimating Time-Inhomogeneous Transition Probabilities from District Level Daily COVID-19 Transmission Data in Sierra Leone
作者： Jah, A., Ngesa, O., Wamwea, C., Ngunyi, A.
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
The study addresses the limitation of conventional compartmental epidemic models that assume fixed transition probabilities between disease states, which does not reflect the dynamic reality of COVID-19 transmission influenced by interventions and variants.
设计与对象
This paper develops a time-inhomogeneous Markov chain framework for the SEIR process and applies it to a compiled daily, district-level COVID-19 surveillance panel for Sierra Leone spanning March 2020 to December 2023, covering 16 districts and 1,401 days.
核心方法
该研究推导了时变转移概率的约束最大似然估计,建立了其统计特性,并提出了一种随机机器学习混合扩展,该扩展使用逻辑回归和随机森林来回归政策和流动性协变量的平滑概率。
主要结果
似然比检验拒绝了 16 个地区中的 15 个地区中的暴露到感染转变以及 16 个地区中的 8 个地区中的感染到去除转变的时间同质性 (α = 0.05),并且协变量增强逻辑模型实现了样本外 Brier 评分,比时间同质基线小大约 76 倍。
解释与创新
The authors interpret these results as statistical evidence that time-inhomogeneous, covariate-informed Markov models provide a materially better description of district-level COVID-19 transmission in Sierra Leone than classical time-homogeneous models.
科研与公卫意义
研究结果对资源有限环境中的地方 暴发 监测具有影响,证明了结合现实世界干预措施和流动性数据的动态模型的价值。
局限与证据强度
证据强度很高,因为该研究使用了大型、全面的数据集和严格的统计方法,尽管其局限性可能包括协变量中未捕获的潜在的未测量的混杂因素。

Estimating Time-Inhomogeneous Transition Probabilities from District Level Daily COVID-19 Transmission Data in Sierra Leone

Authors: Jah, A., Ngesa, O., Wamwea, C., Ngunyi, A.
Original abstract (English)
Abstract: Compartmental epidemic models conventionally treat the probability of moving between dis ease states as fixed over time, an assumption that sits uneasily with the reality of a pandemic in which lockdowns, mask mandates, vaccination roll-out, and the arrival of new variants continually reshape transmission. This paper develops a time-inhomogeneous Markov chain framework for the Susceptible–Exposed–Infectious–Removed (SEIR) process, in which each transition probability pab(t) is allowed to vary with calendar time while respecting the struc tural zeros implied by the SEIR compartmental flow. We derive the constrained maximum likelihood estimator of pab(t) under these structural constraints, establish its finite sample efficiency, asymptotic normality, and Wilson score confidence intervals, and construct a like lihood ratio test of the null hypothesis that a compartment’s exit probability is constant over time. We further propose a stochastic machine learning hybrid extension in which the raw, kernel smoothed transition probabilities are regressed on policy and mobility covariates using both a logistic generalized linear model and a random forest, allowing the framework to attribute time-inhomogeneity to observable interventions. The methodology is applied to a compiled daily, district level COVID-19 surveillance panel for Sierra Leone spanning March 2020 to December 2023 (16 districts, 1,401 days). The likelihood ratio test rejects time-homogeneity of the exposed to infectious transition in 15 of 16 districts and of the infectious-to-removed transition in 8 of 16 districts (α = 0.05), and the covariate augmented logistic model achieves an out of sample Brier score roughly 76 times smaller than a time homogeneous pooled baseline, with healthcare capacity and the time trend emerging as the most influential predictors in the random-forest component. These results provide statisti cal evidence that time-inhomogeneous, covariate informed Markov models offer a materially better description of district-level COVID-19 transmission in Sierra Leone than classical time homogeneous compartmental models, with implications for sub-national outbreak monitoring in resource constrained settings
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the limitation of conventional compartmental epidemic models that assume fixed transition probabilities between disease states, which does not reflect the dynamic reality of COVID-19 transmission influenced by interventions and variants.
Design and population
This paper develops a time-inhomogeneous Markov chain framework for the SEIR process and applies it to a compiled daily, district-level COVID-19 surveillance panel for Sierra Leone spanning March 2020 to December 2023, covering 16 districts and 1,401 days.
Core methods
The study derives a constrained maximum likelihood estimator for time-varying transition probabilities, establishes its statistical properties, and proposes a stochastic machine learning hybrid extension that regresses smoothed probabilities on policy and mobility covariates using logistic regression and random forest.
Main results
The likelihood ratio test rejects time-homogeneity of the exposed-to-infectious transition in 15 of 16 districts and the infectious-to-removed transition in 8 of 16 districts (α = 0.05), and the covariate-augmented logistic model achieves an out-of-sample Brier score roughly 76 times smaller than a time-homogeneous baseline.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these results as statistical evidence that time-inhomogeneous, covariate-informed Markov models provide a materially better description of district-level COVID-19 transmission in Sierra Leone than classical time-homogeneous models.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings have implications for sub-national outbreak monitoring in resource-constrained settings, demonstrating the value of dynamic models that incorporate real-world interventions and mobility data.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is high, as the study uses a large, comprehensive dataset and rigorous statistical methods, though limitations may include potential unmeasured confounders not captured in the covariates.

校对受损的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 的恢复揭示了突变表型和活力的 ExoN 活性阈值。

Recovery of proofreading-impaired SARS-CoV-2 reveals a mutator phenotype and an ExoN activity threshold for viability.
作者： Li He, Yuan-Wei Norman Su, Fushun Zhang, Ibrahim M. Moustafa, David W. Gohara, Chengjin Ye, Martinez-Sobrido L, Jamie J. Arnold, Craig E. Cameron, Yan Xiang
摘要中文翻译摘要 冠状病毒 (CoV) 复制异常大的 RNA 基因组,需要由非结构蛋白 14 (nsp14) 和 10 (nsp10) 形成的 3' 至 5' 核糖核酸酶 (ExoN) 进行校对。先前的研究表明,严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒 2 (严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2) 中 ExoN 催化位点的失活是致命的,但该病毒是否能够耐受受损的校对活性仍悬而未决。在这里,我们通过将基于连续荧光的生化测定与优化的单杆粒反向遗传学系统相结合,研究了 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 复制中 ExoN 的功能要求。对参与 RNA 结合或催化的残基的突变分析揭示了对 ExoN 体外活性的分级影响。Lys9(位于 RNA 结合界面附近的残基)的丙氨酸取代不会降低 ExoN 活性,而该位置 (K9E) 的电荷反转比催化基序 I 残基 D90 和 E92 (D90A/E92A) 的丙氨酸取代更强烈地损害活性。相应地,携带K9A的重组严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2很容易被恢复,而D90A/E92A突变体仅在较长的延迟后才被恢复,而K9E尽管多次尝试仍无法被拯救。D90A/E92A 突变体表现出复制减少,同时在连续传代期间保持工程化的 ExoN 取代。病毒群体的深度测序揭示了 D90A/E92A 突变体中 基因组 全序列变异的显着增加,证明了稳定的突变体表型。总之,这些发现表明 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 可以耐受 ExoN 活性的严重损害,但取决于生存能力的最小活性阈值。该系统提供了一个平台,用于定义 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 校对如何控制 基因组 稳定性、病毒适应性以及对利用降低的复制保真度的 抗病毒药物 策略的敏感性。重要性冠状病毒具有异常大的 RNA 基因组,因为它们编码一种校对酶,可以消除复制过程中的复制错误。目前还不清楚当这种校对功能被强烈削弱时,严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 是否还能生存,因为早期的研究表明,酶催化活性的丧失是致命的。我们表明,严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 可以容忍校对的严重损害,但前提是残余核酸外切酶活性保持在最低阈值以上。校对受损的病毒复制效率较低,并在其 基因组 上累积 突变,而更严重的缺陷会阻止病毒恢复。这些发现阐明了冠状病毒校对如何平衡 基因组 稳定性与病毒适应性,并为研究复制保真度降低如何影响病毒 进化、抗病毒药物 敏感性和衰减提供了有用的系统。定义此活动阈值还可能有助于指导针对冠状病毒校对的 抗病毒药物 策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究探讨了 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 是否可以耐受其 ExoN 酶受损的校对活性,因为之前的研究表明完全失活是致命的,而病毒部分校对受损的能力尚未解决。
设计与对象
该研究采用连续荧光生化检测结合优化的单杆粒反向遗传学系统来研究 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 复制中 ExoN 的功能需求。
核心方法
对参与 RNA 结合或催化的残基进行突变分析,体外评估 ExoN 活性,回收重组 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 突变体,并通过深度测序评估复制能力和 基因组 全序列变异。
主要结果
突变分析显示对ExoN活性的分级影响,K9A突变体未表现出活性降低且易于恢复,D90A/E92A突变体表现出活性降低且延迟恢复,但在连续传代期间保持取代,而K9E突变体尽管多次尝试仍无法恢复;D90A/E92A 突变体表现出稳定的突变体表型,基因组 范围内的序列变异增加。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 可以耐受 ExoN 活性的严重损害,但依赖于生存能力的最小活性阈值,当校对部分受损时,显示出突变表型。
科研与公卫意义
这项研究阐明了冠状病毒校对如何平衡 基因组 稳定性与病毒适应性,为研究复制保真度降低对病毒 进化 和 抗病毒药物 敏感性的影响提供了一个平台,并可能指导针对冠状病毒校对的 抗病毒药物 策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在体外进行的,可能无法完全代表体内条件;然而,直接生化测定和反向遗传学的使用为研究结果提供了很高的可信度。

Recovery of proofreading-impaired SARS-CoV-2 reveals a mutator phenotype and an ExoN activity threshold for viability.

Authors: Li He, Yuan-Wei Norman Su, Fushun Zhang, Ibrahim M. Moustafa, David W. Gohara, Chengjin Ye, Martinez-Sobrido L, Jamie J. Arnold, Craig E. Cameron, Yan Xiang
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Coronaviruses (CoVs) replicate unusually large RNA genomes that necessitate proofreading by the 3′-to-5′ exoribonuclease (ExoN) formed by nonstructural proteins 14 (nsp14) and 10 (nsp10). Previous studies suggested that inactivation of the ExoN catalytic site in severe acute respiratory syndrome CoV 2 (SARS-CoV-2) is lethal, leaving unresolved whether the virus can tolerate impaired proofreading activity. Here, we investigated the functional requirement for ExoN in SARS-CoV-2 replication by combining a continuous fluorescence-based biochemical assay with an optimized single-bacmid reverse genetics system. Mutational analysis of residues involved in RNA binding or catalysis revealed graded effects on ExoN activity in vitro . Alanine substitution of Lys9, a residue positioned near the RNA-binding interface, did not reduce ExoN activity, whereas charge reversal at this position (K9E) impaired activity more strongly than alanine substitutions of the catalytic motif I residues D90 and E92 (D90A/E92A). Correspondingly, recombinant SARS-CoV-2 carrying K9A was readily recovered, whereas the D90A/E92A mutant was recovered only after an extended delay, and K9E could not be rescued despite repeated attempts. The D90A/E92A mutant exhibited reduced replication while maintaining the engineered ExoN substitutions during serial passage. Deep sequencing of viral populations revealed a marked increase in genome-wide sequence variation in the D90A/E92A mutant, demonstrating a stable mutator phenotype. Together, these findings indicate that SARS-CoV-2 can tolerate substantial impairment of ExoN activity but depends on a minimal activity threshold for viability. This system provides a platform for defining how SARS-CoV-2 proofreading controls genome stability, viral fitness, and sensitivity to antiviral strategies that exploit reduced replication fidelity. IMPORTANCE Coronaviruses have unusually large RNA genomes because they encode a proofreading enzyme that removes copying errors during replication. It has been unclear whether SARS-CoV-2 can survive when this proofreading function is strongly weakened because earlier studies suggested that loss of the enzyme’s catalytic activity is lethal. We show that SARS-CoV-2 can tolerate substantial impairment of proofreading but only when residual exonuclease activity remains above a minimal threshold. A virus with impaired proofreading replicates less efficiently and accumulates mutations across its genome, whereas a more severe defect prevents virus recovery. These findings clarify how coronavirus proofreading balances genome stability with viral fitness and provide a useful system for studying how reduced replication fidelity affects viral evolution, antiviral sensitivity, and attenuation. Defining this activity threshold may also help guide antiviral strategies that target coronavirus proofreading.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed whether SARS-CoV-2 can tolerate impaired proofreading activity by its ExoN enzyme, as previous research suggested complete inactivation is lethal, leaving unresolved the virus's capacity for partial proofreading impairment.
Design and population
The research employed a continuous fluorescence-based biochemical assay combined with an optimized single-bacmid reverse genetics system to investigate the functional requirement for ExoN in SARS-CoV-2 replication.
Core methods
Mutational analysis of residues involved in RNA binding or catalysis was performed, with ExoN activity assessed in vitro, and recombinant SARS-CoV-2 mutants were recovered and evaluated for replication capacity and genome-wide sequence variation via deep sequencing.
Main results
Mutational analysis revealed graded effects on ExoN activity, with the K9A mutant showing no activity reduction and being readily recovered, the D90A/E92A mutant exhibiting reduced activity and delayed recovery but maintaining substitutions during serial passage, and the K9E mutant being unrecoverable despite repeated attempts; the D90A/E92A mutant displayed a stable mutator phenotype with increased genome-wide sequence variation.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings indicate that SARS-CoV-2 can tolerate substantial impairment of ExoN activity but depends on a minimal activity threshold for viability, demonstrating a mutator phenotype when proofreading is partially compromised.
Scientific and public-health significance
This research clarifies how coronavirus proofreading balances genome stability with viral fitness, provides a platform for studying reduced replication fidelity's effects on viral evolution and antiviral sensitivity, and may guide antiviral strategies targeting coronavirus proofreading.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in vitro and may not fully represent in vivo conditions; however, the use of direct biochemical assays and reverse genetics provides high confidence in the findings.

鼻内 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 疫苗 加强改变免疫印记和 B 细胞库 2331448

Intranasal SARS-CoV-2 vaccine boosting alters immune imprinting and B cell repertoire 2331448
作者： Xin-Yi Liu, Chieh-Yu Liang, Michael S. Diamond
摘要中文翻译摘要 简介 免疫印记是一种现象,其中先前的抗原暴露将变异特异性从头 免疫应答 限制到随后的相关抗原。小鼠和人类对 Omicron 加强剂的血清反应受到先前编码 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 Wuhan-1 刺突(例如 mRNA-1273)的 疫苗 的强烈影响。包括改变剂量和间隔时间在内的策略在增强变异特异性免疫方面取得的成功有限。鼻内 (i.n.) 疫苗接种 是一种有前景的方法,可引发粘膜和全身免疫。在这里,我们分析了即时交付的变体增强剂的反应。在血清、细胞和 B 细胞库水平上启动 mRNA-1273 后,了解增强途径如何调节印记效应。方法 C57BL/6 小鼠经肌肉注射初免。与 mRNA-1273 和异源加强肌肉注射。使用黑猩猩腺病毒载体 疫苗 (ChAd-严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2-S) 对抗 XBB.1.5 刺突。分析血清和支气管肺泡灌洗液 (BALF) 的交叉反应性和菌株特异性尖峰结合 IgG/IgA。使用假病毒中和测定来评估中和活性。对引流淋巴结 (dLN) 中的交叉反应和菌株特异性尖峰结合记忆 B 细胞 (MBC) 进行了定量。正在进行的研究正在使用单细胞 BCR 测序来分析浆母细胞克隆型,并选择表达为 mAb 的克隆型进行功能和表位分析。结果 针对 XBB.1.5 尖峰的 ChAd-严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2-S 鼻内加强诱导了更高比例的 XBB.1.5 特异性血清 IgG 和 BALF IgA,具有更高的中和活性。鼻内 疫苗接种 增加了 dLN 中的 XBB.1.5 特异性 MBC,表明 B 细胞特异性存在途径依赖性转变。结论 与肌内注射相比,鼻内加强在血清和细胞水平上促进更大的变异特异性反应。正在进行的 B 细胞库和 mAb 分析将为 疫苗接种 途径如何重塑克隆选择和成熟提供机制见解,为合理的 疫苗接种 设计提供信息。资金来源 Moderna 和 NIH R01AI157155 主题类别 疫苗 和免疫疗法 (VAC)
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
免疫印记将变异特异性从头 免疫应答 限制到随后的相关抗原,而改变剂量和间隔等策略在增强变异特异性免疫方面取得的成功有限。
设计与对象
C57BL/6 小鼠用 mRNA-1273 进行肌内引发,并用针对 XBB.1.5 刺突的黑猩猩腺病毒载体 疫苗 进行异源肌内加强。
核心方法
分析血清和支气管肺泡灌洗液中的交叉反应性和毒株特异性尖峰结合 IgG/IgA,使用假病毒中和试验评估中和活性,并对引流淋巴结中的交叉反应性和毒株特异性尖峰结合记忆 B 细胞进行定量。
主要结果
使用针对 XBB.1.5 尖峰的 ChAd-严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2-S 鼻内加强诱导了更高比例的 XBB.1.5 特异性血清 IgG 和 BALF IgA,具有更大的中和活性,并增加了引流淋巴结中的 XBB.1.5 特异性记忆 B 细胞。
解释与创新
与肌内加强相比,鼻内加强在血清和细胞水平上促进更大的变异特异性反应,并且正在进行的 B 细胞库和单克隆抗体分析将为 疫苗接种 途径如何重塑克隆选择和成熟提供机制见解。
科研与公卫意义
The findings inform rational vaccination design by demonstrating that intranasal boosting can overcome immune imprinting and enhance variant-specific immunity, which is crucial for developing more effective COVID-19 vaccines.
局限与证据强度
由于该研究是在小鼠中进行的,因此证据强度中等,需要持续分析才能充分理解其机制;然而,结果为 疫苗 的开发提供了有希望的见解。

Intranasal SARS-CoV-2 vaccine boosting alters immune imprinting and B cell repertoire 2331448

Authors: Xin-Yi Liu, Chieh-Yu Liang, Michael S. Diamond
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction Immune imprinting is a phenomenon in which prior antigen exposure limits variant-specific de novo immune responses to subsequent related antigens. The serum response to Omicron boosters in mice and human are strongly imprinted by prior vaccines encoding SARS-CoV-2 Wuhan-1 spike (e.g., mRNA-1273). Strategies including changes of dosage and interval have had limited success in enhancing variant-specific immunity. Intranasal (i.n.) vaccination is a promising approach that elicits mucosal and systemic immunity. Here we analysed the response of variant boosters delivered i.m. after mRNA-1273 priming at the serum, cellular, and B cell repertoire levels to understand how boosting routes modulate the imprinting effect. Methods C57BL/6 mice were primed i.m. with mRNA-1273 and heterologously boosted i.m. with a chimpanzee adenoviral-vectored vaccine (ChAd-SARS-CoV-2-S) against XBB.1.5 spike. Serum and bronchoalveolar lavage fluid (BALF) were analysed for cross-reactive and strain-specific spike-binding IgG/IgA. Neutralizing activity was assessed using pseudovirus neutralization assays. Cross-reactive and strain-specific spike-binding memory B cells (MBCs) in draining lymph nodes (dLNs) were quantified. Ongoing studies are using single-cell BCR sequencing to analyse plasmablast clonotypes, with selected ones expressed as mAbs for functional and epitope analyses. Results Intranasal boosting with ChAd-SARS-CoV-2-S against XBB.1.5 spike induced higher fractions of XBB.1.5-specific serum IgG and BALF IgA with greater neutralizing activity. Intranasal vaccination increased XBB.1.5-specific MBCs in dLNs, indicating a route-dependent shift in B cell specificity. Conclusion Intranasal boosting promotes greater variant-specific response at both the serum and cellular levels than i.m. Ongoing B cell repertoire and mAb analyses will provide mechanistic insight into how vaccination route reshapes clonal selection and maturation, informing rational vaccination design. Funding Source Moderna and NIH R01AI157155 Topic Categories Vaccines and Immunotherapy (VAC)
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Immune imprinting limits variant-specific de novo immune responses to subsequent related antigens, and strategies like changing dosage and interval have had limited success in enhancing variant-specific immunity.
Design and population
C57BL/6 mice were primed intramuscularly with mRNA-1273 and heterologously boosted intramuscularly with a chimpanzee adenoviral-vectored vaccine against XBB.1.5 spike.
Core methods
Serum and bronchoalveolar lavage fluid were analysed for cross-reactive and strain-specific spike-binding IgG/IgA, neutralizing activity was assessed using pseudovirus neutralization assays, and cross-reactive and strain-specific spike-binding memory B cells in draining lymph nodes were quantified.
Main results
Intranasal boosting with ChAd-SARS-CoV-2-S against XBB.1.5 spike induced higher fractions of XBB.1.5-specific serum IgG and BALF IgA with greater neutralizing activity and increased XBB.1.5-specific memory B cells in draining lymph nodes.
Interpretation and novelty
Intranasal boosting promotes greater variant-specific response at both the serum and cellular levels than intramuscular boosting, and ongoing B cell repertoire and monoclonal antibody analyses will provide mechanistic insight into how vaccination route reshapes clonal selection and maturation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform rational vaccination design by demonstrating that intranasal boosting can overcome immune imprinting and enhance variant-specific immunity, which is crucial for developing more effective COVID-19 vaccines.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate as the study was conducted in mice, and ongoing analyses are needed to fully understand the mechanisms; however, the results provide promising insights for vaccine development.

Prevalence and Determinants of Hospital-Acquired Infections in Intensive Care Units Before and After the COVID-19 Pandemic: A Retrospective Descriptive Surveillance Study at a Tertiary Care Hospital in Iran.

Prevalence and Determinants of Hospital-Acquired Infections in Intensive Care Units Before and After the COVID-19 Pandemic: A Retrospective Descriptive Surveillance Study at a Tertiary Care Hospital in Iran.
作者： Shahla Shahbazi, Saeed Mohammadi, Niloofar Heidari, Mohammad Amin Rezaei, Maria Shirvani
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
This study aimed to investigate the prevalence and determinants of hospital-acquired infections (HAIs) in intensive care units (ICUs) before and after the COVID-19 pandemic, as HAIs cause significant morbidity, mortality, and costs.
设计与对象
对 2019 年至 2020 年伊朗克尔曼沙赫 Taleghani 医院收治的 ICU 患者进行了一项回顾性描述性分析研究。该研究比较了大流行前后伊朗三级医院的 HAI 患病率、类型和病原体。
核心方法
从医院记录中收集人口统计、临床和微生物学数据。使用卡方或 Fisher 精确检验评估分类变量之间的关联,并使用 Mann-Whitney U 检验比较连续非正态分布变量,p < 0.05 被认为具有统计显着性。
主要结果
在 1778 例入住 ICU 的患者中,发现了 301 例 HAI 病例,总体 患病率 为 16.9%。患病率 从 2019 年的 131/718 (18.2%) 下降到 2020 年的 170/1060 (16.0%)。虽然 2020 年 VAE 和肺炎有所下降,但 UTI 和 BSI 却有所增加。
解释与创新
作者解释说,尽管在大流行期间加强了感染控制,但 HAI,特别是革兰氏阴性菌感染和 VAE,在 ICU 中仍然普遍存在。这项研究为大流行期间 HAI 的变化模式提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调,需要持续采取 监测 和有针对性的干预措施,以减轻 ICU 中 HAI 的负担,特别是在当前大流行的背景下。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和对医院记录的依赖而受到限制,这可能会引入偏差。证据强度中等,因为结果基于观察数据和统计关联。

Prevalence and Determinants of Hospital-Acquired Infections in Intensive Care Units Before and After the COVID-19 Pandemic: A Retrospective Descriptive Surveillance Study at a Tertiary Care Hospital in Iran.

Authors: Shahla Shahbazi, Saeed Mohammadi, Niloofar Heidari, Mohammad Amin Rezaei, Maria Shirvani
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Background and Aims Hospital‐acquired infections (HAIs) in intensive care units (ICUs) cause significant morbidity, mortality, and costs. The COVID‐19 pandemic intensified infection control, but its effect on HAI prevalence and patterns is unclear. This study compared HAI prevalence, types, and causative agents in an Iranian tertiary hospital before and after COVID‐19. Methods A retrospective descriptive‐analytical study was conducted on ICU patients admitted to Taleghani Hospital, Kermanshah, Iran, between 2019 and 2020. Demographic, clinical, and microbiological data were collected from hospital records. Associations between categorical variables were assessed using Chi‐square or Fisher's exact tests, and continuous non‐normally distributed variables were compared using the Mann–Whitney U test, with p < 0.05 considered statistically significant. Results Among 1778 ICU admissions, 301 cases of HAIs were identified, yielding an overall prevalence of 16.9%. The prevalence decreased from 131/718 (18.2%) in 2019 to 170/1060 (16.0%) in 2020. Gram‐negative aerobic bacteria were the predominant pathogens in both years (128/131 [97.7%] vs. 163/170 [95.9%]), while fungal and other infections emerged only in 2020. Ventilator‐associated events (VAE) were the most frequent infection type, followed by pneumonia, urinary tract infections (UTIs), surgical site infections (SSIs), and bloodstream infections (BSIs). While VAE and pneumonia decreased in 2020, UTIs and BSIs increased. ICU2 consistently reported the highest infection rates, whereas ICU3 experienced a marked rise in infections in 2020. Statistical analysis confirmed significant associations between infection occurrence and gender, ICU ward, and Gram‐negative bacterial infections (p < 0.05). Conclusion Despite strengthened infection control during COVID‐19, HAIs particularly Gram‐negative infections and VAE remain prevalent in ICUs. Continuous surveillance and targeted interventions are essential to reduce their burden.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the prevalence and determinants of hospital-acquired infections (HAIs) in intensive care units (ICUs) before and after the COVID-19 pandemic, as HAIs cause significant morbidity, mortality, and costs.
Design and population
A retrospective descriptive-analytical study was conducted on ICU patients admitted to Taleghani Hospital, Kermanshah, Iran, between 2019 and 2020. The study compared HAI prevalence, types, and causative agents in an Iranian tertiary hospital before and after the pandemic.
Core methods
Demographic, clinical, and microbiological data were collected from hospital records. Associations between categorical variables were assessed using Chi-square or Fisher's exact tests, and continuous non-normally distributed variables were compared using the Mann–Whitney U test, with p < 0.05 considered statistically significant.
Main results
Among 1778 ICU admissions, 301 cases of HAIs were identified, yielding an overall prevalence of 16.9%. The prevalence decreased from 131/718 (18.2%) in 2019 to 170/1060 (16.0%) in 2020. While VAE and pneumonia decreased in 2020, UTIs and BSIs increased.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that despite strengthened infection control during the pandemic, HAIs, particularly Gram-negative infections and VAE, remain prevalent in ICUs. The study provides novel insights into the changing patterns of HAIs during the pandemic.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for continuous surveillance and targeted interventions to reduce the burden of HAIs in ICUs, especially in the context of the ongoing pandemic.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and reliance on hospital records, which may introduce bias. The evidence strength is moderate, as the results are based on observational data and statistical associations.

感染后血管炎皮肤病变中持续存在的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 刺突蛋白 或 mRNA 疫苗接种:回顾性病例对照免疫荧光研究。

Persistent SARS-CoV-2 Spike Protein in Vasculitic Skin Lesions after Infection or mRNA Vaccination: A Retrospective Case control Immunofluorescence Study.
作者： Andrea Gawaz, Anneli Yilmaz, Birgit Fehrenbacher, Simon Riel, Jule Riebelmann, Elena Hagelauer, Michael Schindler, Matthias Hahn, Sebastian Volc, Martin Schaller
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
据报道,血管炎性皮肤病变与 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染和 疫苗接种 相关,但病毒蛋白在急性期后是否持续存在于病变血管中仍不清楚。
设计与对象
This retrospective case-control study includes 9 patients with persistent or delayed-onset vasculitic skin lesions following COVID-19 infection (n=4) or mRNA vaccination (n=5), with prepandemic leukocytoclastic vasculitis (n=3) and healthy postinfection/postvaccination skin (n=3) serving as controls.
核心方法
使用 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 刺突蛋白 的免疫荧光分析皮肤活检。
主要结果
Spike protein was detected in vascular endothelial cells of all 9 patients with COVID-19- or vaccine-associated vasculitis, but staining was absent in all prepandemic vasculitis and healthy control samples.
解释与创新
These findings suggest that retained viral proteins may contribute to prolonged or delayed-onset vasculitic skin manifestations, demonstrating that SARS-CoV-2 spike protein can persist in endothelial cells of vasculitic skin lesions weeks to months after COVID-19 infection or mRNA vaccination.
科研与公卫意义
该研究为 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染或 疫苗接种 后持续性血管炎皮肤表现的潜在机制提供了证据,这可能为此类病例的未来诊断方法和治疗策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
需要进一步的研究来阐明尖峰持续性的机制及其临床意义;由于样本量小和回顾性设计,证据强度中等。

Persistent SARS-CoV-2 Spike Protein in Vasculitic Skin Lesions after Infection or mRNA Vaccination: A Retrospective Case control Immunofluorescence Study.

Authors: Andrea Gawaz, Anneli Yilmaz, Birgit Fehrenbacher, Simon Riel, Jule Riebelmann, Elena Hagelauer, Michael Schindler, Matthias Hahn, Sebastian Volc, Martin Schaller
Original abstract (English)
Vasculitic skin lesions have been reported in association with both SARS-CoV-2 infection and vaccination. Whether viral proteins persist in lesional vessels beyond the acute phase remains unclear. The aim of this study was to investigate the presence of SARS-CoV-2 spike protein in persistent or delayed-onset cutaneous vasculitis temporally associated with COVID-19 infection or vaccination. This retrospective case-control study includes 9 patients with persistent or delayed-onset vasculitic skin lesions following COVID-19 infection (n=4) or mRNA vaccination (n=5). Skin biopsies were analysed using immunofluorescence for SARS-CoV-2 spike protein. Prepandemic leukocytoclastic vasculitis (n=3) and healthy postinfection/postvaccination skin (n=3) served as controls. Spike protein was detected in vascular endothelial cells of all 9 patients with COVID-19- or vaccine-associated vasculitis. Staining was absent in all prepandemic vasculitis and healthy control samples. Histopathology showed typical features of leukocytoclastic vasculitis without evidence of viral cytopathic changes. We found that SARS-CoV-2 spike protein can persist in endothelial cells of vasculitic skin lesions weeks to months after COVID-19 infection or mRNA vaccination. These findings suggest that retained viral proteins may contribute to prolonged or delayed-onset vasculitic skin manifestations. Further studies are needed to clarify the mechanisms underlying spike persistence and its clinical significance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Vasculitic skin lesions have been reported in association with both SARS-CoV-2 infection and vaccination, but whether viral proteins persist in lesional vessels beyond the acute phase remains unclear.
Design and population
This retrospective case-control study includes 9 patients with persistent or delayed-onset vasculitic skin lesions following COVID-19 infection (n=4) or mRNA vaccination (n=5), with prepandemic leukocytoclastic vasculitis (n=3) and healthy postinfection/postvaccination skin (n=3) serving as controls.
Core methods
Skin biopsies were analysed using immunofluorescence for SARS-CoV-2 spike protein.
Main results
Spike protein was detected in vascular endothelial cells of all 9 patients with COVID-19- or vaccine-associated vasculitis, but staining was absent in all prepandemic vasculitis and healthy control samples.
Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance
The study provides evidence for a potential mechanism underlying persistent vasculitic skin manifestations after SARS-CoV-2 infection or vaccination, which may inform future diagnostic approaches and therapeutic strategies for such cases.
Limitations and evidence strength
Further studies are needed to clarify the mechanisms underlying spike persistence and its clinical significance; the evidence strength is moderate due to the small sample size and retrospective design.

Omicron 时代尼日利亚东南部农村社区未接种疫苗的成年人中 血清流行率 和 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染的危险因素:一项基于社区的横断面研究

Seroprevalence and risk factors for SARS-CoV-2 infection among unvaccinated adults in a rural community in South-East Nigeria during the Omicron era: a community-based cross-sectional study
作者： Chizaram Onyeaghala, Uche Unigwe, Nneka Chika-Igwenyi, Michael Iroezindu
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估 Omicron 时代尼日利亚东南部农村社区未接种疫苗的成年人中的 血清流行率 和 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染的危险因素,解决撒哈拉以南非洲地区缺乏基于人群的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 暴露数据的问题。
设计与对象
2022 年 3 月 11 日至 6 月 12 日,在尼日利亚东南部埃努古州对 252 名同意的未接种疫苗的成年人进行了一项基于社区的横断面研究。252 名参与者中有 39 名 (15.48%) 同时具有 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 IgG 和 IgM 抗体。
核心方法
使用酶联免疫吸附测定测试每位参与者的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 IgM 和 IgG 抗体。使用多变量逻辑回归分析来确定与 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 血清阳性独立相关的因素。
主要结果
The seroprevalence of SARS-CoV-2 (IgG or IgM) was 67.90%. Multivariable logistic regression analysis showed that healthcare workers had significantly higher odds of SARS-CoV-2 seropositivity than non-healthcare workers (adjusted odds ratio [aOR] = 3.83, 95% confidence interval [CI]: 1.52–9.66; P = 0.004).
解释与创新
严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 IgG 相对于 IgM 抗体的优势表明大多数感染发生在样本采集之前,并且与研究期间 传播 社区的下降相一致。
科研与公卫意义
在大约三分之二的未接种疫苗的成年人中观察到 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染的血清学证据,表明到 2022 年上半年,研究人群中通过自然感染广泛存在先前的暴露。
局限与证据强度
P 值 < 0.05 被认为具有统计显着性。该研究是在一个农村社区进行的,这可能会限制普遍性。

Seroprevalence and risk factors for SARS-CoV-2 infection among unvaccinated adults in a rural community in South-East Nigeria during the Omicron era: a community-based cross-sectional study

Authors: Chizaram Onyeaghala, Uche Unigwe, Nneka Chika-Igwenyi, Michael Iroezindu
Original abstract (English)
Severe Acute Respiratory Syndrome Coronavirus-2 (SARS-CoV-2) infection is an emerging viral disease that became a global pandemic in March 2020. Despite the protracted pandemic, population-based local data on the burden and exposure risks of SARS-CoV-2 infection remain limited in most parts of sub-Saharan Africa, where the pandemic surprisingly defied early public health predictions. A community-based cross-sectional study of 252 consenting unvaccinated adults was conducted from March 11 to June 12, 2022, in Enugu State, southeastern Nigeria. A two-stage cluster sampling design was used, with a validated, interviewer-administered questionnaire adapted from the World Health Organization Unity Study Protocol, to collect demographic, socio-behavioural, and clinical data. Each participant was tested for SARS-CoV-2 IgM and IgG antibodies by the enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay. Multivariate analysis was used to determine factors independently associated with SARS-CoV-2 seropositivity. A P-value of < 0.05 was considered statistically significant. The mean age was 47.72 ± 17.92 years, with 57.14% (144) female. The seroprevalence of SARS-CoV-2 (IgG or IgM) was 67.90%. Thirty-nine (15.48%) out of 252 participants had both SARS-CoV-2 IgG and IgM antibodies. Multivariable logistic regression analysis showed that healthcare workers had significantly higher odds of SARS-CoV-2 seropositivity than non-healthcare workers (adjusted odds ratio [aOR] = 3.83, 95% confidence interval [CI]: 1.52–9.66; P = 0.004). Serological evidence of SARS-CoV-2 infection was observed in approximately two out of every three unvaccinated adults, indicating widespread previous exposure through natural infection in the study population by the first half of 2022. The predominance of SARS-CoV-2 IgG over IgM antibodies suggests that most infections occurred before sample collection and is consistent with declining community transmission during the study period. Healthcare worker status was independently associated with SARS-CoV-2 seropositivity, reinforcing the importance of strengthening infection prevention and control measures to protect frontline healthcare workers during future emerging infectious disease outbreaks.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to assess the seroprevalence and risk factors for SARS-CoV-2 infection among unvaccinated adults in a rural community in South-East Nigeria during the Omicron era, addressing the lack of population-based data on SARS-CoV-2 exposure in sub-Saharan Africa.
Design and population
A community-based cross-sectional study of 252 consenting unvaccinated adults was conducted from March 11 to June 12, 2022, in Enugu State, southeastern Nigeria. Thirty-nine (15.48%) out of 252 participants had both SARS-CoV-2 IgG and IgM antibodies.
Core methods
Each participant was tested for SARS-CoV-2 IgM and IgG antibodies using the enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay. Multivariable logistic regression analysis was used to determine factors independently associated with SARS-CoV-2 seropositivity.
Main results
The seroprevalence of SARS-CoV-2 (IgG or IgM) was 67.90%. Multivariable logistic regression analysis showed that healthcare workers had significantly higher odds of SARS-CoV-2 seropositivity than non-healthcare workers (adjusted odds ratio [aOR] = 3.83, 95% confidence interval [CI]: 1.52–9.66; P = 0.004).
Interpretation and novelty
The predominance of SARS-CoV-2 IgG over IgM antibodies suggests that most infections occurred before sample collection and is consistent with declining community transmission during the study period.
Scientific and public-health significance
Serological evidence of SARS-CoV-2 infection was observed in approximately two out of every three unvaccinated adults, indicating widespread previous exposure through natural infection in the study population by the first half of 2022.
Limitations and evidence strength
A P-value of < 0.05 was considered statistically significant. The study was conducted in a single rural community, which may limit generalisability.

Identifying the Most Important Influencing Factors Based on Demographic Information, Nutrition, Physical Activity, Leisure Time, and Underlying Diseases in the Severity of Disease in COVID-19 Patients Using Data Mining Techniques.

Identifying the Most Important Influencing Factors Based on Demographic Information, Nutrition, Physical Activity, Leisure Time, and Underlying Diseases in the Severity of Disease in COVID-19 Patients Using Data Mining Techniques.
作者： Noushin Mohammadifard, Nizal Sarafzadegan, Fahimeh Haghighat Doost, Jamshid Najafian, Mohammad Hossein Rouhani, Gholamreza Askari, Mohammad Sattari
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
The study aimed to identify the most important influencing factors in the severity of disease in COVID-19 patients using demographic information, nutrition, physical activity, leisure time, and underlying diseases. The COVID-19 pandemic has affected millions of individuals worldwide and resulted in substantial mortality.
设计与对象
This study evaluates the performance of three machine learning approaches—Deep Learning, Gradient Boosted Decision Trees (GBDT), and Support Vector Machine (SVM)—in identifying factors associated with COVID-19 severity. The study uses data mining techniques to analyze comprehensive patient data, facilitating the identification of key patterns and determinants that support clinical and preventive decision-making.
核心方法
数据挖掘和机器学习技术可以分析全面的患者数据,促进识别支持临床和预防决策的关键模式和决定因素。基于 ROC 曲线和定量性能指标的比较分析表明,深度学习和 GBDT 优于 SVM。
主要结果
所有模型一致将高血压视为主要危险因素。然而,其他影响变量也存在差异:GBDT 将高血压列为最重要的因素,其次是胃溃疡,而深度学习则强调维生素摄入量是主要决定因素。
解释与创新
深度学习树变体进一步支持了维生素消耗的重要性。深度学习和 GBDT 的卓越预测性能提供了可靠的基准并产生新颖的见解,包括胃溃疡和维生素摄入量对疾病严重程度的潜在作用。
科研与公卫意义
These findings emphasize the value of employing multiple high-performing models and robust validation strategies for accurate identification of clinical risk factors in COVID-19. The study provides insights into the potential roles of specific variables in disease severity, which can inform targeted interventions and clinical decision-making.
局限与证据强度
提供的证据没有报告研究设计或样本量的具体限制。证据仅基于抽象数据,研究绩效的评估仅限于机器学习模型的比较。

Identifying the Most Important Influencing Factors Based on Demographic Information, Nutrition, Physical Activity, Leisure Time, and Underlying Diseases in the Severity of Disease in COVID-19 Patients Using Data Mining Techniques.

Authors: Noushin Mohammadifard, Nizal Sarafzadegan, Fahimeh Haghighat Doost, Jamshid Najafian, Mohammad Hossein Rouhani, Gholamreza Askari, Mohammad Sattari
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Background and Aims The COVID‐19 pandemic has affected millions of individuals worldwide and resulted in substantial mortality. Data mining and machine learning techniques enable the analysis of comprehensive patient data, facilitating the identification of key patterns and determinants that support clinical and preventive decision‐making. Methods This study evaluates the performance of three machine learning approaches—Deep Learning, Gradient Boosted Decision Trees (GBDT), and Support Vector Machine (SVM)—in identifying factors associated with COVID‐19 severity. A comparative analysis based on ROC curves and quantitative performance metrics demonstrates that Deep Learning and GBDT outperform SVM. Results All models consistently identify hypertension as a major risk factor. However, differences emerge in other influential variables: GBDT ranks hypertension as the most significant factor, followed by stomach ulcers, whereas Deep Learning highlights vitamin intake as the primary determinant. The deep learning tree variant further supports the importance of vitamin consumption. Conclusion The superior predictive performance of Deep Learning and GBDT provides a reliable benchmark and yields novel insights, including the potential roles of stomach ulcers and vitamin intake in disease severity. These findings emphasize the value of employing multiple high‐performing models and robust validation strategies for accurate identification of clinical risk factors in COVID‐19.
